HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_05c532538c1e1d469bc3","text":"2. **The drink is forced internalization of aversion.** `س ق ي B001` supplies giving drink, while the less expected `س ق ي B010` supplies forcing dislike into the heart. The passive `يُسْقَى` is followed in 14:17 by tiny gulps (`ج ر ع B001`) that cannot pass smoothly (`س و غ B001`). The packet gives compulsory administration and bodily refusal; I infer that the punishment forces the rejected material inward. Drinking therefore reads as coerced incorporation, not refreshment with a bad substance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_07f027711b9ee1c8e773","text":"Ayah 14:32's life-giving descent and waterways supplied the strongest correction: the drink here is a deliberately inverted provision chain, a route to water that no longer nourishes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001642/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_174a278f85cd85c1aaed","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:16:2\",\"root_001642\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ardındaki cehennem, sabit bir yönün gerisi değil, her aşamanın ötesinde bekleyen ve tekrar açılan kaçınılmaz bir ufuktur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B001","root_000848/B004","root_001458/B001","root_001642/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2eaad402bf4d39b8fa93","text":"1. **What is “beyond” closes in through an inverted act of watering.** `و ر ي` `B006` gives the far side as behind, ahead, or beyond; `س ق ي` `B001` is giving drink; `م و ه` `B001` is water; and `ص د د` `B004` unexpectedly supplies a road to water alongside `B001` turning aside. The later provision sequence uses water to bring out fruit, while here the route culminates in purulent drink. I infer punishment reverses provisioning: what should sustain arrives from the unavoidable beyond as corrupted intake. *Waraʾihi* remains spatially double, an unseen future already surrounding him.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001642/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3980cf193cefefa5064c","text":"1. **`وَرَاء` is an awaiting far side, not simply the rear.** `و ر ي B006` supplies the far side as behind, ahead, or beyond. The failed tyrant of 14:15 has hell beyond his present horizon, and 14:17 repeats `مِن وَرَائِهِ` after a first ordeal to place another punishment beyond it. The packet supplies recursive spatial sequencing; I infer temporal anticipation from it. Hell reads as what awaits on the far side of the current scene, not as a fixed direction behind the person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_42260d1938de7d47bd73","text":"14:17 supplied both retrospective surprises: it repeated `وَرَاء` after an initial ordeal, creating a recursive horizon, and its failed swallowing activated `س ق ي B010` as forced internalization of aversion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7f24ca1149dc72e0c398","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:16:4\",\"root_000722\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İçirilme, hoşnutsuzluğu zorla içe yerleştiren bir eylem niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B001","root_000848/B004","root_001458/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b454369a85583dc133df","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:16:4\",\"root_000722\",[\"B001\"]],[\"14:16:6\",\"root_001458\",[\"B001\"]],[\"14:16:7\",\"root_000848\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hayat veren su ve ürün zinciri burada beslemeyen, bozulmuş bir alıma dönüştürülerek tersine çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000848/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c1886a3a43b6c656c479","text":"3. **A liquid named by `ص د د` echoes enacted turning-away.** `ص د د B001` supplies turning away or aside. Within this focus window, the opponents have turned from authorization and tried to expel the messengers; now `صَدِيد` is packet-rooted in the same consonantal field and is made the object of forced drinking. I infer, exploratorily, that refusal becomes materialized and returned inward. The weak link is that the accepted inventory offers no bodily-fluid branch for this surface, so this is a root-image activation rather than a claim about its ordinary lexical sense.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000848/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ef67e8d3fee3f6f4c20f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:16:7\",\"root_000848\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"İrinli içecek, daha önceki yüz çevirmenin maddileşip içeri döndürülmesi olarak okunabilir; ancak bu bağ olağan anlamı açıklamaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
